| **Multi-tenancy** | Many customers, logically isolated |

---

Exam Objective 4.2
بۇلۇت ھېسابلاش ئۇقۇملىرىنى خۇلاسىلەڭ.

# 3. Cloud Service Types

Cloud service لار source بويىچە ئۈچ primary category غا بۆلۈنىدۇ:

1. **IaaS — Infrastructure as a Service**
2. **PaaS — Platform as a Service**
3. **SaaS — Software as a Service**

بۇ ئۈچ model:
- abstraction level
- functionality
- user vs provider responsibility

جەھەتتە پەرقلىنىدۇ.

## IaaS — Infrastructure as a Service

**IaaS**
- foundational cloud layer
- fundamental computing infrastructure resource تەمىنلەيدۇ

Source resources:
- servers
- storage
- networking infrastructure

User:
- virtual machine قۇرالايدۇ
- network قۇرالايدۇ
- remote management قىلالايدۇ
- demand غا قاراپ scale قىلالايدۇ

Source-stated benefit:
- costly physical hardware غا investment ئېھتىياجىنى ئازايتىش
- infrastructure maintenance burden نى ئازايتىش
- flexible environment for data/application hosting

**Exam memory: IaaS = Rent infrastructure**

## PaaS — Platform as a Service

**PaaS**
- software development
- application deployment

ئۈچۈن managed platform/environment تەمىنلەيدۇ.

Source analogy:
> **Streamlined construction site for developers**

Provider:
- underlying server configuration
- networking resource management

قاتارلىق infrastructure detail لارنى abstraction قىلىدۇ.

Developer:
- coding
- building applications
- deploying applications

قا مەركەزلەشەلەيدۇ.

Source-stated benefits:
- faster development lifecycle
- rapid application delivery
- team collaboration

**Exam memory: PaaS = Build/deploy apps without managing underlying infrastructure**

## SaaS — Software as a Service

**SaaS**
- ready-made software application نى Internet ئارقىلىق ئىشلەتكۈچىگە تەمىنلەش

Source analogy:
> **Fully furnished home**

Access:
- web browser
- dedicated client

Source examples/categories:
- productivity suites
- CRM — Customer Relationship Management systems

Source-stated benefits:
- local installation burden نى ئازايتىش
- maintenance burden نى ئازايتىش
- update burden نى ئازايتىش
- convenience
- accessibility

**Source wording note:** transcript بۇ جۈملىدە grammatical error بار؛ reviewed Uyghur intended meaning نى natural شەكىلدە بېرىدۇ: **users use/consume ready-made applications over the Internet**.

**Exam memory: SaaS = Use finished software**

# Shared Responsibility

Cloud computing دا:
- user
- cloud service provider

ئارىسىدا management/security/operation responsibility بۆلۈنىدۇ.

Source components:
- data center
- networking
- storage
- servers
- virtualization
- operating system
- data
- applications

## IaaS Responsibility — Source Model

### Provider
- data center
- networking
- storage
- server maintenance
- virtualization management

### User
- operating system
- applications
- data

**Exam memory: IaaS = User manages more**

## PaaS Responsibility — Source Model

Provider:
- underlying infrastructure
- operating system

نىمۇ manage قىلىدۇ.

User:
- application development
- application deployment
- application management
- data

غا مەسئۇل.

**Exam memory: PaaS = Provider manages platform; user manages apps/data**

## SaaS Responsibility — Source Model

Provider:
- source wording بويىچە infrastructure دىن application غىچە **majority / entire stack** نى manage قىلىدۇ.

User:
- application settings/configuration
- user access

نى manage قىلىدۇ.

**Exam memory: SaaS = Provider manages most; user configures/uses app**

## Shared Responsibility Comparison

| Layer / responsibility | IaaS | PaaS | SaaS |
|---|---|---|---|
| Data center | Provider | Provider | Provider |
| Networking | Provider | Provider | Provider |
| Storage | Provider | Provider | Provider |
| Servers | Provider | Provider | Provider |
| Virtualization | Provider | Provider | Provider |
| Operating system | **User** | **Provider** | Provider |
| Applications | **User** | **User** | Provider |
| Data | **User** | **User** | Source says provider manages most stack; user still uses/configures application |
| App settings / user access | User-side management | User-side management | **User** |

### Source-Scope Caution

بۇ table transcript نىڭ own responsibility model ىنى study-friendly شەكىلدە كۆرسىتىدۇ.

Reviewed Uyghur:
- vendor-specific shared-responsibility matrix
- security/compliance exception
- identity/network-control nuance
- customer-data ownership detail

نى سىرتقى مەنبەدىن قوشمايدۇ.

---

# Objective 4.2 — Final Exam Memory Map

## Cloud Models
- **Public** = Third-party provider / scalable Internet resources
- **Private** = One organization / dedicated control
- **Hybrid** = Public + Private
- **Community** = Organizations with shared needs

## Characteristics
- **Shared resources** = Resource pooling
- **Dedicated resources** = One tenant
- **Metered utilization** = Pay-as-you-go
- **Rapid elasticity** = Quickly scale up/down
- **High availability** = Redundancy + fault tolerance + failover
- **File synchronization** = Latest files across devices
- **Multi-tenancy** = Multiple tenants + logical isolation

## Service Types
- **IaaS** = Infrastructure
- **PaaS** = Development/deployment platform
- **SaaS** = Ready-made software

## Responsibility
- **IaaS** → User manages OS + apps + data
- **PaaS** → User manages apps + data
- **SaaS** → Provider manages most; user handles app settings/user access in source model

## One-Line Memory
> **IaaS: Build on it → PaaS: Develop on it → SaaS: Use it**

============================================================
Exam Objective 5.1
بىر ئەھۋال بېرىلگەندە، ئانا تاختا، RAM، CPU ۋە توك مەسىلىلىرىنى كاشىلا تۈگىتىڭ.

# Objective 5.1 — Troubleshooting Mindset

مەنبە بۇ objective نىڭ بېشىدا ناھايىتى مۇھىم test-taking rule بېرىدۇ:

> **Repair ياكى component replacement قىلىشتىن بۇرۇن، ئالدى بىلەن verify / inspect / check قىلىڭ.**

**Transcript correction:** source `as a protest taking tip` دەپ transcript قىلىنغان. Context بويىچە reviewed Uyghur دا **pro test-taking tip** مەنىسى ساقلىنىدۇ.

Objective 5.1 دىكى ئاساسىي troubleshooting flow:

1. Symptom نى توغرا تونۇڭ
2. Simple checks نى ئالدى بىلەن قىلىڭ
3. Possible root cause لارنى ئايرىڭ
4. Hardware نى inspect/test قىلىڭ
5. ئاندىن repair/replacement نى ئويلاڭ

---

# 1. Computer Power On بولمايدۇ

## Symptom

- Power button باسسىمۇ system قوزغالمايدۇ
- Fan/LED/POST response بولماسلىقى مۇمكىن

## Source-Stated Possible Causes

### Faulty PSU

**PSU — Power Supply Unit**
- system component لارغا electrical power تەمىنلەيدۇ

Faulty PSU:
- adequate power يەتكۈزەلمەسلىكى
- ياكى پۈتۈنلەي fail بولۇشى

مۇمكىن.

نەتىجە:
- system power on بولمايدۇ

### Loose Power Connections

Loose connection:
- PSU cable ↔ motherboard
- PSU cable ↔ CPU
- PSU cable ↔ other components

توك ئېقىمىنى ئۈزۈشى مۇمكىن.

### No Power from Wall Outlet

PSU نىڭ ئۆزىمۇ:
- wall outlet دىن power ئالمايۋاتقان بولۇشى مۇمكىن

## First Checks

مەنبەنىڭ opening troubleshooting rule ىغا ماس:

- wall power بارمۇ؟
- PSU/cable connection loose ئەمەسمۇ؟
- motherboard/CPU power connection ئورنىدامۇ؟
- ئاندىن PSU fault نى تەكشۈرۈڭ

**Exam memory: No power → Outlet → Connections → PSU**

---

# 2. POST / Beep Codes

ئەگەر computer يېتەرلىك power ئالسا:
- **POST — Power-On Self-Test**

غا ئۆتىدۇ.

Source teaching model:
- POST OK → single audible beep
- hardware problem → beep sequence

**Source-model caution:** beep behavior motherboard/firmware implementation غا باغلىق بولۇشى مۇمكىن. Reviewed Uyghur بۇنى transcript نىڭ exam teaching model ى سۈپىتىدە ساقلايدۇ، universal hardware rule دەپ كېڭەيتمەيدۇ.

## Beep Codes نىڭ مەقسىتى

Beep pattern:
- specific hardware problem غا clue بېرىدۇ

Source common culprit لار:
- motherboard
- memory/RAM
- CPU

## Motherboard Checks

مەنبە motherboard نى تۆۋەندىكى defect لارغا inspect قىلىشنى تىلغا ئالىدۇ:

- chipset failure
- damaged capacitor
- corrosion
- loose component

## RAM Checks

RAM:
- failed بولغانمۇ؟
- unseated / slot دىن چىقىپ قالغانمۇ؟

## CPU Checks

CPU:
- CPU failure بارمۇ؟
- CPU fan disconnected بولغانمۇ؟

**Exam memory: Beep codes → Motherboard / RAM / CPU clues**

---

# 3. Black Screen at Startup

## Symptom

Computer power بار، ئەمما:
- display دا image چىقمايدۇ
- startup black screen

## Source-Stated Possible Causes

### Motherboard Problem
- POST نى باشلىتىشقا توسالغۇ بولۇشى مۇمكىن
- ياكى beep code پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن

### Graphics Card Problem
- graphics output fail بولۇشى مۇمكىن

### Display Problem
- monitor/display side issue بولۇشى مۇمكىن

### PSU Problem
- power supply issue black screen غا تۆھپە قوشۇشى مۇمكىن

**Exam memory: Black screen → Motherboard / Graphics / Display / PSU**

---

# 4. Proprietary Crash Screens

System:
- power on
- POST دىن ئۆتتى
- black screen يوق

بولسا boot process دا operating system load قىلىدۇ.

مەنبە بۇ باسقۇچتا **proprietary crash screen** كۆرۈلۈشى مۇمكىن دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

Source examples:
- Windows → **Blue Screen of Death (BSOD)**
- macOS → **spinning pinwheel**

## Possible Cause 1 — Driver Conflict

- incompatible driver
- outdated driver

operating system بىلەن conflict قىلىپ:
- instability
- crash

پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Faulty Hardware

Source examples:
- damaged motherboard
- failing RAM
- malfunctioning graphics card
- overheating CPU

**Exam memory: Crash screen → Drivers OR faulty/overheating hardware**

---

# 5. Overheating

Overheating:
- system stability
- performance
- hardware health

غا ئېغىر تەسىر قىلىدۇ.

## Source-Stated Symptoms/Effects

- erratic behavior
- system instability
- unexpected shutdown
- hardware damage

## Possible Cause 1 — Poor Cooling / Airflow

Source examples:
- insufficient airflow
- inadequate ventilation
- poorly designed cooling system

## Possible Cause 2 — Dust and Debris

Dust/debris:
- internal component ئۈستىگە يىغىلىدۇ
- heat dissipation نى توسۇشى مۇمكىن

## Possible Cause 3 — Fan Failure

Fan:
- optimal speed دا ئىشلىمەسلىكى
- ياكى پۈتۈنلەي توختاپ قېلىشى

system temperature نى تېز ئۆستۈرىدۇ.

**Exam memory: Overheating → Airflow / Dust / Fan**

---

# 6. Burning Smell

## Symptom

Computer ئىچىدىن:
- burning smell

كېلىشى ناھايىتى ئېغىر warning sign.

Source meaning:
- overheating
- internal hardware damage
- imminent hardware failure

## Possible Sources

مەنبە almost any hardware component cause بولالايدىغانلىقىنى ئېيتىدۇ، مىسال:

- PSU
- motherboard
- overheating CPU

## Immediate Action — Source Safety Step

Burning smell بولسا:

1. **System نى دەرھال power off قىلىڭ**
2. **Power source دىن unplug قىلىڭ**
3. **Professional assistance / diagnosis ئىزدەڭ**

Ignore قىلىش:
- irreversible damage
- data loss
- fire hazard

غا ئېلىپ كېلىشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Burning smell → Power off + Unplug immediately**

---

# 7. Sluggish Performance

## Symptom

System:
- ناھايىتى ئاستا
- response ناچار
- productivity تۆۋەن

## Possible Cause 1 — Insufficient Compute Resources

Source examples:
- CPU processing power يېتەرلىك ئەمەس
- available RAM ئاز
- available storage ئاز

## Possible Cause 2 — CPU Overheating

CPU temperature بەك يۇقىرى بولسا:
- CPU **throttle** قىلىشى
- processing speed نى تۆۋەنلىتىشى

مۇمكىن.

مەقسەت:
- hardware damage نىڭ ئالدىنى ئېلىش

**Exam memory: Sluggish → CPU/RAM/Storage resources OR CPU thermal throttling**

---

# 8. Random Shutdowns

## Symptom

Computer ئىشلىتىۋاتقاندا:
- warning يوق
- تۇيۇقسىز ئۆچۈپ قالىدۇ

## Possible Cause 1 — Faulty PSU

- unstable power delivery
- PSU failure

random shutdown پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Insufficient PSU Wattage

PSU wattage:
- system power requirement غا يەتمىسە

shutdown پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 3 — Overheating

Critical component:
- CPU
- graphics card

temperature بەك يۇقىرى بولسا:
- system damage نىڭ ئالدىنى ئېلىش ئۈچۈن automatic shutdown قىلىشى مۇمكىن

**Exam memory: Random shutdown → PSU / Wattage / Overheating**

---

# 9. Frequent Application Crashes

## Symptom

Application لار:
- قايتا-قايتا crash
- unexpected close
- malfunction

## Possible Cause 1 — Faulty RAM

Defective RAM:
- data corruption
- unexpected application crash

پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Software Conflicts

Source examples:
- incompatible software versions
- conflicting drivers
- corrupted system files

## Possible Cause 3 — Insufficient Storage

Available disk space ئاز بولسا:
- application
- operating system

نىڭ نورمال ئىشلىشىگە توسالغۇ بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: App crashes → RAM / Software conflicts / Low storage**

---

# 10. Unusual Noise

## Symptom

Computer ئىچىدىن:
- grinding
- rattling
- buzzing

ئاۋازلىرى كېلىدۇ.

## Source-Stated Common Cause — Fan

Fan:
- broken
- failing
- obstructed
- dust بىلەن clogged
- worn out

بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

مەنبە fan نىڭ:
- CPU
- PSU

غا ئوخشاش critical component لارنى cooling قىلىدىغانلىقىنى تەكىتلەيدۇ.

**Exam memory: Grinding/Rattling/Buzzing → Check fan**

---

# 11. Swollen / Bulging Capacitors

## Capacitor دېگەن نېمە؟

Source بويىچە capacitor:
- motherboard ۋە باشقا circuit board لاردا بار
- electrical energy نى store/release قىلىدۇ

Source-stated functions:
- voltage stabilization
- current regulation

## Symptom

Capacitor:
- swollen
- bulging

بولسا serious hardware issue.

## Source-Stated Causes

- excessive heat
- prolonged usage
- manufacturing defects

Internal pressure:
- capacitor نى كېڭەيتىدۇ

## Possible Effects

- motherboard circuitry disruption
- instability
- erratic behavior
- complete system failure

Severe case:
- electrolytic fluid leakage
- smoke
- burning smell

**Exam memory: Bulging capacitor = Motherboard hardware failure warning**

---

# 12. Incorrect System Date / Time — CMOS Battery Clue

Transcript بۇ بۆلەكتە **CMOS** بىلەن **CMOS battery** نىڭ رولىنى بىر جۈملىدە ئارىلاشتۇرۇپ تەسۋىرلەيدۇ.

Reviewed Uyghur exam point نى ئېنىق ئايرىيدۇ:

### CMOS / BIOS Settings Context

Source:
- BIOS setting
- system date/time
- hardware configuration

power off بولغاندا ساقلىنىشى كېرەك

دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

### CMOS Battery

Source troubleshooting clue:
- **CMOS battery** backup power تەمىنلەيدۇ
- main power disconnected بولغاندىمۇ date/time/settings نى ساقلاشقا ياردەم بېرىدۇ

Battery:
- degrade
- charge يوقىتىش

قىلسا:
- system date
- system time

خاتا بولۇپ قېلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Why Wrong Date/Time Matters

Source examples:
- file timestamping
- software licensing
- scheduled tasks

تەسىرگە ئۇچرىشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Date/time keeps resetting → Check CMOS battery**

**Source-accuracy note:** transcript بۇ جايىدا CMOS hardware term بىلەن battery نى بىرلەشتۈرۈپ تەسۋىرلەيدۇ. Reviewed lesson بۇنى literal hardware definition سۈپىتىدە تەكرارلىمايدۇ؛ source نىڭ ئېنىق troubleshooting clue ى — **CMOS battery preserves date/time/settings** — نىلا study point قىلىدۇ.

---

# Objective 5.1 — Symptom → Cause Memory Table

| Symptom | Source-stated likely causes/checks |
|---|---|
| **No power** | Wall outlet, loose power cable, PSU |
| **POST/beep codes** | Motherboard, RAM, CPU / CPU fan |
| **Black screen** | Motherboard, graphics card, display, PSU |
| **Crash screen** | Driver conflict, faulty hardware, overheating CPU |
| **Overheating** | Airflow, ventilation, dust, fan |
| **Burning smell** | PSU/motherboard/CPU or other damaged hardware; power off/unplug |
| **Sluggish performance** | CPU/RAM/storage resources, CPU overheating/throttling |
| **Random shutdowns** | Faulty PSU, insufficient wattage, overheating |
| **Frequent app crashes** | Faulty RAM, software conflict, low storage |
| **Grinding/rattling/buzzing** | Fan problem |
| **Bulging capacitor** | Motherboard/circuit-board failure warning |
| **Wrong date/time** | CMOS battery |

---

# Objective 5.1 — Final Exam Rules

1. **Check before replace.**
2. **No power → start with power source/connections/PSU.**
3. **Beep codes → motherboard/RAM/CPU clues.**
4. **Black screen ≠ automatically display only; motherboard/GPU/PSU can also be involved.**
5. **Overheating → airflow + dust + fan.**
6. **Burning smell → shut down and unplug immediately.**
7. **Sluggish performance → resources or thermal throttling.**
8. **Random shutdown → power or heat.**
9. **Frequent crashes → RAM/software/storage.**
10. **Grinding/rattling/buzzing → fan.**
11. **Bulging capacitor → serious board warning.**
12. **Date/time resets → CMOS battery.**

============================================================
Exam Objective 5.2
بىر ئەھۋال بېرىلگەندە، ساقلاش دىسكىسى ۋە RAID مەسىلىلىرىنى كاشىلا تۈگىتىڭ.

# Objective 5.2 — Troubleshooting Mindset

مەنبە Objective 5.1 دىكىگە ئوخشاش يەنە بىر قېتىم مۇھىم troubleshooting rule نى تەكىتلەيدۇ:

> **Repair ياكى component replacement قىلىشتىن بۇرۇن، verify / inspect / check قىلىڭ.**

Storage drive ۋە RAID array:
- data ساقلاش
- data باشقۇرۇش
- system stability
- performance

ئۈچۈن مۇھىم.

مەسىلە كۆرۈلسە:
- data loss
- corruption
- degraded performance
- system instability

پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

---

# 1. LED Status Indicators

Storage drive ياكى RAID controller ئۈستىدىكى **LED status indicator**:
- drive/array health
- activity
- warning state

ھەققىدە visual clue بېرىدۇ.

مەنبەدىكى typical examples:

### Steady Green
- normal operation نى كۆرسىتىشى مۇمكىن

### Flashing Red
- drive failure غا warning بولۇشى مۇمكىن
- faulty disk نى ئالماشتۇرۇش ۋە data recovery/rebuild action نى تەلەپ قىلىشى مۇمكىن

### Amber / Orange
- cautionary state نى كۆرسىتىشى مۇمكىن

### Blinking Pattern
- ongoing data-transfer activity نى كۆرسىتىشى مۇمكىن

**Source-model caution:** LED color/pattern meaning hardware/vendor غا قاراپ پەرقلىنىدۇ. Reviewed Uyghur source دا ئىشلىتىلگەن `typically / might` wording نى ساقلايدۇ؛ بۇ رەڭلەرنى universal rule دەپ كۆرسەتمەيدۇ.

**Exam memory: LED = Quick visual health/activity clue**

---

# 2. Audible RAID / Storage Alarms

**Audible alarm**
- RAID controller ياكى storage system critical issue بايقىغاندا چىقىرىدىغان warning sound

Source use:
- server room
- data center
- visually continuously monitored بولمىغان environment

## Common Source Causes

### Failed Drive in RAID Array
- repeating alarm چىقىشى مۇمكىن
- array **degraded state** دا ئىشلەۋاتقان بولۇشى مۇمكىن
- failed drive نى ئالماشتۇرۇش كېرەك بولۇشى مۇمكىن

### Other Possible Causes
- overheating component
- power-supply problem
- controller issue

Quick response:
- failing component نى ئالماشتۇرۇش
- RAID array نى rebuild قىلىش
- further disruption/data loss نى ئازايتىش

**Exam memory: RAID alarm → Failed drive / Heat / Power / Controller**

---

# 3. HDD Grinding / Clicking / Ticking

Traditional **HDD** mechanical parts ئىشلىتىدۇ:
- spinning platters
- moving read/write heads

## Symptom

HDD دىن:
- grinding
- repetitive clicking
- ticking

ئاۋازلىرى كېلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Source-Stated Possible Causes

- malfunctioning read/write head
- damaged platter
- physical wear/damage

بۇ noise:
- drive نىڭ data نى توغرا read/write قىلىشتا قىينىلىۋاتقانلىقى
- complete failure غا يېقىنلىشىۋاتقانلىقى

نىڭ serious clue ى بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Action

Grinding/clicking ئاڭلانسا:

1. **Critical data نى دەرھال backup قىلىڭ**
2. **Failing drive نى ئالماشتۇرۇشقا تەييارلىنىڭ**

**Exam memory: HDD grinding/clicking → Backup first + Prepare replacement**

---

# 4. Data Loss / Data Corruption

## Possible Source Causes

- failed storage drive
- corrupted file system

## Recovery Options — Source

مەنبە:
- data recovery tools
- professional recovery services

نى تىلغا ئالىدۇ.

مەقسەت:
- lost/corrupted data نى salvage قىلىش
- impact نى ئازايتىش

## Prevention — Regular Backups

مەنبەنىڭ ئەڭ كۈچلۈك prevention rule ى:

> **Regular backups = Primary defense against data loss/corruption**

## Disk Diagnostic Utilities

Source examples:
- Windows → **check disk command**
- macOS → **file system consistency check**

مەنبە بۇ tool لارنى:
- file-system integrity check
- underlying disk/file-system error نى بايقاش/تۈزىتىشكە ياردەم بېرىش

بىلەن باغلايدۇ.

**Source-boundary note:** transcript command abbreviation/flags بەرمەيدۇ؛ reviewed lesson `chkdsk`, `fsck` ياكى command syntax نى سىرتتىن قوشمايدۇ.

**Exam memory: Data loss/corruption → Recovery attempt + Backups + File-system checks**

---

# 5. RAID Failure / Degraded Array

**RAID — Redundant Array of Independent Disks**

مەنبە RAID نىڭ:
- redundancy
- performance improvement

تەمىنلىيەلەيدىغانلىقىنى ئەسكەرتىدۇ، ئەمما RAID نىڭ ئۆزىمۇ failure غا ئۇچرىشى مۇمكىن.

## Common Problem — Disk Failure

Array ئىچىدىكى بىر drive fail بولسا:
- data loss/corruption risk
- degraded array state
- slower data access/performance

پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Recovery Flow

RAID failure دا source تۆۋەندىكى general flow نى چۈشەندۈرىدۇ:

1. Failed drive نى identify قىلىڭ
2. Failed drive نى new drive بىلەن ئالماشتۇرۇڭ
3. **RAID rebuild / data reconstruction** باشلاڭ
4. Redundancy نى ئەسلىگە كەلتۈرۈڭ

**Source-scope note:** transcript RAID level-specific rebuild procedure ياكى hot-swap rule بەرمەيدۇ؛ reviewed lesson بۇلارنى قوشمايدۇ.

**Exam memory: Degraded RAID → Diagnose → Replace failed drive → Rebuild**

---

# 6. SMART Failure Warning

**SMART — Self-Monitoring, Analysis and Reporting Technology**

Source wording:
- modern storage drive ئىچىدىكى health-monitoring system
- drive health/performance metric لارنى continuously monitor قىلىدۇ
- potential failure نى ئالدىن بايقاشقا تىرىشىدۇ

## SMART vs File-System Check

### Check Disk / File-System Consistency Check
- mainly file-system integrity غا قارايدۇ

### SMART
- drive-health/performance metric لارنى monitor قىلىدۇ
- impending hardware failure clue نى بايقىشى مۇمكىن

## SMART Warning كۆرۈلسە

Source recommended action:

1. **Critical data نى دەرھال backup قىلىڭ**
2. **Affected drive نى ئالماشتۇرۇڭ**

مەقسەت:
- data loss نى ئازايتىش
- system downtime نى ئازايتىش

**Exam memory: SMART failure warning → Backup immediately + Replace drive**

---

# 7. Extended / Slow Read and Write Times

## Symptom

Storage drive:
- read operation ئاستا
- write operation ئاستا
- system performance ناچار

## Source Cause — HDD Fragmentation

Traditional HDD دا:
- file data disk ئۈستىدە small pieces قىلىپ تارقىلىپ كەتسە

**fragmentation** بولىدۇ.

نەتىجە:
- read time ئۇزىرىشى
- read/write efficiency تۆۋەنلىشى

مۇمكىن.

## Free Space — Source Claim

Transcript:
- drive دا sufficient free space بولسا
- continuous block ئۈچۈن room كۆپىيىپ
- fragmentation نى ئازايتىشقا ياردەم بېرىشى مۇمكىن

دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

Reviewed Uyghur بۇنى source claim سۈپىتىدەلا ساقلايدۇ.

## Defragmentation

Source:
- **disk defragmentation**
- scattered data نى continuous blocks قا قايتا تەرتىپلەيدۇ
- read time نى ئازايتىش
- overall performance نى ياخشىلاش

بىلەن باغلايدۇ.

**Source-boundary note:** بۇ segment defragmentation نى HDD fragmentation context دا بېرىدۇ؛ reviewed lesson SSD optimization/defrag rule نى سىرتتىن قوشمايدۇ.

**Exam memory: Slow HDD + fragmentation → Defragmentation may help**

---

# 8. IOPS — Input/Output Operations Per Second

**IOPS**
- storage device بىر سېكۇنتتا قانچە read/write operation نى بىر تەرەپ قىلالايدىغانلىقىنى ئۆلچەيدىغان performance metric

Source expands:
> **IOPS = Input/Output Operations Per Second**

## Source-Stated Inputs

1. **Average operation size**
   - ھەر read/write operation دىكى data amount
   - ئادەتتە bytes بىلەن ئۆلچىنىدۇ

2. **Transfer rate**
   - storage device بىر سېكۇنتتا read/write قىلالايدىغان data amount

## Source Formula

Transcript نىڭ simplified formula سى:

> **IOPS = Transfer rate ÷ Average operation size**

**Source-model caution:** بۇ formula course نىڭ simplified teaching model ى. Reviewed Uyghur ئۇنى ھەر storage workload ئۈچۈن universal real-world IOPS formula دەپ كېڭەيتمەيدۇ.

**Exam memory: IOPS = Read/write operations per second**

---

# 9. Drive OS دا كۆرۈنمەيدۇ

## Symptom
- storage drive operating system drive list دا يوق

## Possible Cause 1 — Disconnected Cable
- loose/disconnected physical connection

## Possible Cause 2 — Drive Failure
- failed drive OS تەرىپىدىن detect قىلىنماسلىقى مۇمكىن

## Possible Cause 3 — Initialization / Preparation State

Transcript `drive initialization` نى broad preparation process سۈپىتىدە چۈشەندۈرىدۇ:
- partition table create
- file system format

Source says this is especially relevant for:
- new drive
- previously different system دا ئىشلىتىلگەن drive

Initialization/preparation بولمىسا:
- drive OS available-drive list دا كۆرۈنمەسلىكى مۇمكىن

**Source-accuracy note:** source بۇ يەردە `initialization` نى partition-table creation ۋە formatting نى ئۆز ئىچىگە ئالغان broad process سۈپىتىدە ئىشلىتىدۇ. Reviewed lesson بۇ wording نى source model دەپ ساقلايدۇ.

**Exam memory: Missing drive → Cable / Drive failure / Initialization-preparation**

---

# 10. Bootable Device Not Found

ئەگەر missing drive:
- operating system files بار boot drive

بولسا، source example error:

> **Bootable device not found**

## Possible Source Causes

### Boot Drive Connection
- loose/disconnected storage cable

### Boot Drive Failure
- drive malfunction/damage

### BIOS Boot Order
- correct boot device selected مۇ؟
- boot order توغرىمۇ؟

نى verify قىلىڭ.

**Exam memory: Bootable device not found → Connection / Drive failure / BIOS boot order**

---

# 11. Missing / Offline / Degraded RAID Array

**Transcript correction:** source بۇ final section دا كۆپ قېتىم:
- `rate array`
- `rate controller`

دەپ transcript قىلىنغان.

Context ۋە objective بويىچە reviewed Uyghur canonical:
- **RAID array**
- **RAID controller**

نى ئىشلىتىدۇ.

## Symptom

System ياكى RAID controller:
- array دىكى بىر ياكى بىر نەچچە drive نى detect قىلالمايدۇ

Storage system:
- array missing
- offline
- degraded

دەپ report قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Source Causes

- failed/disconnected member drive
- loose/damaged data cable
- loose power connection
- RAID controller issue
- corrupted RAID configuration

## Source First Steps

1. **All drives properly connected مۇ؟**
2. **All drives power ئېلىۋاتامدۇ؟**
3. **RAID controller interface / management software دا ھەر drive status نى تەكشۈرۈڭ**
4. Underlying issue نى تېز address قىلىڭ

مەقسەت:
- array نى restore قىلىش
- potential data loss risk نى ئازايتىش

**Exam memory: Missing RAID → Connections + Power + Drive status + Controller/config**

---

# Objective 5.2 — Symptom → Cause / Action Table

| Symptom | Source-based clue/action |
|---|---|
| **LED abnormal** | Check drive/RAID health/status; colors/patterns are source examples |
| **RAID audible alarm** | Failed drive, heat, PSU, controller |
| **HDD grinding/clicking** | Mechanical failure → backup + prepare replacement |
| **Data loss/corruption** | Failed drive/file system → recovery + backups + diagnostics |
| **Degraded RAID** | Failed disk → replace + rebuild |
| **SMART failure warning** | Backup immediately + replace affected drive |
| **Slow HDD reads/writes** | Fragmentation/free-space issue in source → defragmentation |
| **Low storage performance** | IOPS is the source performance metric |
| **Drive missing in OS** | Cable / failed drive / initialization-preparation |
| **Bootable device not found** | Boot drive cable / drive failure / BIOS boot order |
| **RAID missing/offline/degraded** | Drive/cable/power/controller/configuration |

# Objective 5.2 — Final Exam Rules

1. **Check before replace.**
2. **LED/alarm = early storage-health warnings.**
3. **Grinding/clicking HDD = serious mechanical warning → backup immediately.**
4. **Regular backups are the source’s strongest prevention against data loss.**
5. **Degraded RAID → identify failed drive → replace → rebuild.**
6. **SMART failure warning → backup + replace drive.**
7. **Fragmented HDD → longer read/write times; source recommends defragmentation.**
8. **IOPS = operations per second; source uses transfer-rate ÷ operation-size model.**
9. **Missing drive → connection / failure / preparation state.**
10. **Bootable device not found → boot drive + BIOS boot order.**
11. **Missing RAID → check drive connections/power and RAID controller status first.**

============================================================
Exam Objective 5.3
بىر ئەھۋال بېرىلگەندە، video، projector ۋە display مەسىلىلىرىنى كاشىلا تۈگىتىڭ.

# Objective 5.3 — Troubleshooting Mindset

مەنبە يەنە بىر قېتىم مۇھىم CompTIA troubleshooting rule نى تەكىتلەيدۇ:

> **Repair ياكى component replacement قىلىشتىن بۇرۇن، verify / inspect / check قىلىڭ.**

Display device لار:
- monitor
- projector
- باشقا video/display system

classroom، conference room ۋە media environment دا مۇھىم.

Objective 5.3 دىكى ھەر symptom نى:
1. symptom
2. possible cause
3. source check/action

شەكلىدە ئوقۇش ئەڭ ئاسان.

---

# 1. Incorrect Input Source

## Symptom

Projector ياكى monitor دا:
- image يوق
- ياكى **No input signal detected**

دېگەن message چىقىشى مۇمكىن.

## Common Source Cause

User:
- wrong input source نى تاللىغان

بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Action

- display/input settings نى verify قىلىڭ
- correct input source نى تاللاڭ

**Exam memory: No signal → Check input source first**

---

# 2. Damaged / Loose Display Cabling

Physical cabling issue:
- image distortion
- audio distortion
- complete signal loss

پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Source-Stated Possible Problems

- loose cable
- damaged cable
- damaged connector
- damaged adapter
- frayed wire
- **bent pins**
- worn-out connector

**Transcript correction:** source `bed pins` دەپ transcript قىلىنغان؛ context بويىچە reviewed Uyghur **bent pins** نى ئىشلىتىدۇ.

## Source Action

- بارلىق cable/connection نى careful inspect قىلىڭ
- damage/wear بارمۇ تەكشۈرۈڭ

**Exam memory: Distorted/no signal → Inspect cables, connectors, adapters**

---

# 3. Dim Image

## Symptom

Display image:
- بەك قاراڭغۇ
- normal دىن significantly dim

## Possible Cause 1 — Brightness Setting

Incorrect brightness:
- source device
- display device

دا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Failed Backlight

Modern monitor/display دا:
- **backlight**

panel نى يورۇتۇپ image نى كۆرۈنەرلىك قىلىدۇ.

Backlight fail بولسا:
- dim screen
- ياكى completely dark screen

پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 3 — Inverter Problem

مەنبە:
- older LCD display
- inverter backlight قا power تەمىنلەيدۇ

دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

Inverter fail بولسا:
- backlight غا inadequate power
- dimness
- flickering

پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Flashlight Test — Source Method

Source troubleshooting method:
1. Screen غا بىۋاسىتە light shine قىلىڭ
2. Faint image كۆرۈنەمدۇ؟

ئەگەر faint image كۆرۈنسە:
- image itself display قىلىنىۋاتقان
- ئەمما backlight يورۇتالمىغان
- backlight ياكى ئۇنىڭ power side ىدا problem بولۇشى مۇمكىن

**Exam memory: Dim screen + faint image under flashlight → Backlight/power clue**

---

# 4. Display Burn-In

**Burn-in**
- persistent image/pattern screen دا ghost image بولۇپ قېلىشى

Source explanation:
- certain pixels static image غا prolonged exposure قىلىنىدۇ
- ئۇلار surrounding pixels دىن تېزرەك degrade بولىدۇ

## Prevention — Source

- static image نى ئۇزاق كۆرسەتمەسلىك
- screensaver ئىشلىتىش
- power-saving feature ئىشلىتىش
- inactivity دا display نى dim/off قىلىش
- content نى rotate قىلىش
- dynamic wallpaper ئىشلىتىش

**Exam memory: Burn-in = Long static image exposure**

---

# 5. Dead Pixels

**Dead pixel**
- individual pixel illuminate قىلمايدۇ
- black spot بولۇپ قالىدۇ
- displayed content قانداق بولسىمۇ dark قالىدۇ

## Source-Stated Causes

- physical panel damage
- prolonged use

## Source Prevention

- display نى careful handle قىلىش
- excessive pressure دىن ساقلىنىش
- liquid دىن يىراق تۇتۇش

**Exam memory: Dead pixel = Individual black non-lighting pixel**

---

# 6. Flashing / Flickering Screen

## Symptom

Display:
- rapidly visible ↔ black
- flicker/flash intermittent

## Possible Cause 1 — Cable / Connector Problem

- poor cable connection
- loose video cable
- damaged connector
- damaged adapter

Signal interruption پەيدا قىلىپ:
- flicker
- flash

قىلدۇرىشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Electrical / Backlight / Inverter Problem

Display ئىچىدىكى:
- backlight
- inverter

malfunction بولسا irregular power supply پەيدا قىلىپ screen flicker/flash قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Action

- cable securely connected مۇ؟
- damage بارمۇ؟
- backlight/inverter issue بارمۇ؟

تەكشۈرۈڭ.

**Exam memory: Flickering → Cable/connection OR backlight/inverter**

---

# 7. Distorted / Washed-Out / Inaccurate Colors

## Symptom

Colors:
- distorted
- washed out
- inaccurate
- unbalanced

## Possible Cause 1 — Faulty Cable

Source examples:
- HDMI
- VGA

Cable damage:
- signal degradation
- interference
- color distortion

پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

Check:
- wear
- fraying
- damaged connector pins

## Possible Cause 2 — Misconfigured Color Settings

Check:
- brightness
- contrast
- hue
- saturation

display ياكى source device دا توغرا تەڭشەلگەنمۇ؟

## Possible Cause 3 — Display Hardware Issue

Malfunctioning display component:
- especially source-described LCD area issue

incorrect color غا سەۋەپ بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Bad colors → Cable → Color settings → Display hardware**

---

# 8. Sizing / Scaling / Aspect Ratio Problems

## Symptom

Image:
- stretched
- compressed
- screen boundary غا fit بولمايدۇ
- edge دىن سىرتقا چىقىدۇ
- ياكى بەك كىچىك بولۇپ unused space قالدۇرىدۇ

## Possible Cause 1 — Incorrect Resolution

Operating system resolution:
- display نىڭ native/recommended resolution ىغا ماس كەلمىسە

image توغرا scale بولماسلىقى مۇمكىن.

Source action:
- recommended/native resolution غا adjust قىلىش

## Possible Cause 2 — Scaling / Aspect Ratio

Incorrect:
- scaling setting
- aspect ratio setting

OS ياكى display/projector دا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

Projector/monitor:
- stretch
- zoom

mode ئىشلىتىپ image نى force-fill قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Wrong size/proportion → Resolution + Scaling + Aspect ratio**

---

# 9. Projector Intermittent Shutdown

## Symptom

Projector operation جەريانىدا:
- unexpected shutdown
- intermittent power-off

## Common Source Cause — Overheating

Overheating cause:
- dust/debris accumulation
- dirty air filter
- restricted airflow
- cooling effectiveness تۆۋەنلىشى

**Transcript correction:** source `dirty air filter, enduring proper airflow` دەپ transcript قىلىنغان. Context بويىچە reviewed Uyghur بۇنى **hindering proper airflow** مەنىسىدە بېرىدۇ.

## Source Maintenance / Checks

- projector air filter نى clean قىلىش
- adequate ventilation بارمۇ؟
- power connection نى inspect قىلىش
- overheating نى rule out قىلغاندىن كېيىن internal component لارنى inspect قىلىش

**Exam memory: Projector shuts down → Heat / Dust / Filter / Ventilation**

---

# 10. Burnt-Out Projector Bulb

## Symptom

Projector:
- no image projected

## Source Cause

Projector bulb:
- time بىلەن deteriorate بولىدۇ
- brightness تۆۋەنلەيدۇ
- ئاخىرى fail بولىدۇ

## Source Action

- projector bulb نى replace قىلىڭ
- manufacturer guideline غا ئەگىشىڭ

**Exam memory: Projector no image + old bulb → Check/replace bulb**

---

# 11. Fuzzy / Blurry Projector Image

## Symptom

Projected image:
- fuzzy
- blurry
- not sharp

## Possible Cause 1 — Lens Out of Focus

Source action:
- projector lens focus نى adjust قىلىڭ

## Possible Cause 2 — Resolution Mismatch

Check:
- projector resolution
- source-device resolution

Source action:
- ئىككى تەرەپتىكى resolution setting نى verify/adjust قىلىڭ

**Exam memory: Fuzzy projector → Focus + Resolution**

---

# 12. Distorted Projector Image / Keystone Distortion

## Symptom

Projected image:
- stretched
- warped
- uneven
- one side wider
- other side narrower
- trapezoid shape

## Source Cause — Improper Projector Placement

Projector:
- projection surface قا angle بىلەن قارىسا
- screen غا perpendicular بولمىسا

**keystone distortion** پەيدا بولىدۇ.

### Keystone Distortion

Source definition:
- image rectangle بولۇشنىڭ ئورنىغا trapezoid shape غا ئۆزگىرىدۇ

## Best Source Fix — Physical Repositioning

Source recommendation:
1. Projector نى center قىلىڭ
2. Level قىلىڭ
3. Lens نى screen center غا بىۋاسىتە aimed قىلىڭ
4. Projector نى projection surface قا تېخىمۇ perpendicular قىلىڭ

## Keystone Correction Setting

Modern projector:
- digital **keystone correction**

feature ئىشلىتىشى مۇمكىن.

ئۇ:
- image top/bottom نى widen/narrow قىلىپ
- rectangular shape قا يېقىنلاشتۇرىدۇ

## Important Source Preference

Transcript:
- digital keystone correction ياردەم بېرىدۇ
- ئەمما physical placement نى correct قىلىش generally better

دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

Reason:
- proper alignment → more accurate image
- heavy digital correction → potential quality loss

**Exam memory: Trapezoid projector image → Reposition first; keystone correction second**

---

# 13. Audio Issues with Display / Projector

## Symptoms

- distorted audio
- no sound

## Possible Source Causes

- incorrect audio settings
- wrong audio-output device
- faulty cable
- speaker/output-device problem

## Source Troubleshooting Flow

### 1. Check Audio Settings
- output configuration توغرىمۇ؟
- correct output device selected مۇ؟

### 2. Inspect Cables
- damage
- wear

بارمۇ؟

Faulty cable:
- signal loss
- distortion
- no sound

پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

### 3. Troubleshoot Speakers / Output Hardware

Source methods:
- speaker نى another audio source بىلەن test قىلىش
- alternative output device نى connect قىلىپ test قىلىش

مەقسەت:
- problem speaker تەرەپتىمۇ؟
- audio source تەرەپتىمۇ؟

ئايرىش.

**Exam memory: Audio problem → Settings → Cable → Speaker/output device**

---

# Objective 5.3 — Symptom → Cause / Action Table

| Symptom | Source-based clue/action |
|---|---|
| **No input signal** | Verify correct input source |
| **Image/audio distortion or no signal** | Inspect cables/connectors/adapters |
| **Dim image** | Brightness / backlight / older-LCD inverter |
| **Ghost image / burn-in** | Long static-image exposure |
| **Black spot / dead pixel** | Physical panel damage / prolonged use |
| **Flashing/flickering** | Cable/connector or backlight/inverter |
| **Bad colors** | Cable / color settings / display hardware |
| **Wrong image size** | Resolution / scaling / aspect ratio |
| **Projector shuts down** | Overheating / dust / dirty filter / ventilation |
| **Projector no image** | Burnt-out bulb |
| **Fuzzy projector image** | Focus / resolution |
| **Trapezoid projector image** | Physical placement / keystone |
| **No/distorted audio** | Audio settings / cable / speaker-output device |

---

# Objective 5.3 — Final Exam Rules

1. **No signal → input source first.**
2. **Distorted/no signal → inspect physical cabling.**
3. **Dim screen → brightness, backlight, older-LCD inverter.**
4. **Burn-in → prolonged static content.**
5. **Dead pixel → one pixel stays black.**
6. **Flicker → cable/connection or backlight/inverter.**
7. **Wrong colors → cable, settings, hardware.**
8. **Sizing → native/recommended resolution + scaling/aspect ratio.**
9. **Projector shutdown → overheating/airflow/filter.**
10. **Projector no image → bulb can be failed.**
11. **Fuzzy image → focus + resolution.**
12. **Trapezoid image → reposition projector before relying heavily on keystone correction.**
13. **Audio issue → settings → cable → speakers/output device.**

============================================================
Exam Objective 5.4
بىر ئەھۋال بېرىلگەندە، كۆچمە ئۈسكۈنىلەردە كۆپ ئۇچرايدىغان مەسىلىلەرنى كاشىلا تۈگىتىڭ.

# Objective 5.4 — Troubleshooting Mindset

مەنبە بۇ objective دا يەنە بىر قېتىم مۇھىم CompTIA troubleshooting rule نى تەكىتلەيدۇ:

> **Repair ياكى component replacement قىلىشتىن بۇرۇن، ئالدى بىلەن verify / inspect / check قىلىڭ.**

Mobile device لار:
- smartphone
- tablet
- touchscreen laptop
- باشقا portable device

بولۇپ، charging، battery، port، display/touch، wireless connection، performance ۋە application مەسىلىلىرىگە ئۇچرىشى مۇمكىن.

بۇ reviewed lesson ھەر مەسىلىنى:

1. **Symptom**
2. **Possible source cause**
3. **Source check/action**
4. **Exam memory**

شەكلىدە تەشكىللەيدۇ.

---

# 1. Improper Charging

## Symptom

Mobile device:
- charge بولمايدۇ
- ناھايىتى ئاستا charge بولىدۇ
- charging connection تۇراقسىز

## Possible Cause 1 — Bad Charging Cable

Damaged/faulty cable:
- charger دىن device قا electrical power flow نى توسۇشى مۇمكىن

## Possible Cause 2 — Poor Port Connection

Charging cable بىلەن device port ئارىسىدىكى connection:
- loose
- ياكى unstable

بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

Port ئىچىدىكى:
- dust
- debris

مۇ connection غا توسالغۇ بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 3 — Mismatched Power Rating

Transcript charger بىلەن device ئارىسىدىكى power rating mismatch نى possible cause سۈپىتىدە بېرىدۇ.

Source says:
- insufficient charger power → slow charging ياكى failure to charge
- higher-than-required power rating → battery غا ۋاقىت ئۆتۈشى بىلەن damage risk پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن

**Source-framing caution:** reviewed Uyghur بۇنى transcript نىڭ own teaching claim ى سۈپىتىدە ساقلايدۇ؛ modern charging negotiation/protocol behavior نى سىرتتىن قوشمايدۇ ياكى بۇ claim نى universal rule دەپ كېڭەيتمەيدۇ.

**Exam memory: Charging problem → Cable → Port/connection/debris → Power rating**

---

# 2. Poor Battery Health

## Source-Stated Causes

### Long-Term Usage / Charge Cycles
Battery:
- repeatedly charge/discharge بولغانسېرى
- ۋاقىت ئۆتۈشى بىلەن degrade بولۇشى مۇمكىن

### Battery Aging
Battery age ئۆسكەنسېرى:
- charge hold قىلىش capacity سى تۆۋەنلەيدۇ
- battery life/performance تۆۋەنلەيدۇ

### Improper Charging
Source:
- improper charging battery decline نى تېزلىتىشى مۇمكىن

دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

## Warning Clues

- device hot to the touch
- charge نى ساقلىيالمايدۇ

بۇلار source بويىچە poor battery health نىڭ serious clues ى.

**Exam memory: Poor battery health → Aging / charge cycles / charging problems**

---

# 3. Swollen Battery — Critical Safety Issue

**Swollen battery**
- device casing دا bulge پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن
- device overheating بىلەن بىللە كۆرۈلۈشى مۇمكىن

Source explanation:
- internal chemical reaction
- gas buildup
- battery expansion

## Physical Clues

Battery expansion device نى deformation قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

Source examples:
- trackpad/button نى نورمال click قىلالماسلىق
- keyboard key نى بېسىش قىيىنلىشىش
- device flat surface ئۈستىدە level تۇرماسلىق
- wall outlet قا plugged in بولغاندا overheating كۆرۈلۈشى

## Source Safety Action

Swollen battery suspected بولسا:

1. **Device نى ئىشلىتىشنى توختىتىڭ**
2. **Professional assistance ئىزدەڭ**
3. **Battery replacement نى promptly address قىلىڭ**

Source warns ignored swollen battery may lead to:
- leakage
- fire
- explosion

**Exam memory: Swollen battery = Stop using device + Professional battery replacement**

---

# 4. Physically Damaged Charging / I/O Port

## Symptom

Device:
- charge connection establish قىلالمايدۇ
- accessories/peripherals نى port ئارقىلىق ئىشلىتەلمەيدۇ

## Source-Stated Causes

Port damage:
- mishandling
- accidental impact
- moisture exposure

بىلەن مۇناسىۋەتلىك بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## First Source Check

> **Known-good / replacement cable بىلەن test قىلىڭ.**

مەقسەت:
- cable faulty مۇ؟
- port faulty مۇ؟

ئايرىش.

**Exam memory: Suspected bad port → Test another cable first**

---

# 5. Mobile Device Overheating

Battery problem overheating نىڭ بىر source cause ى بولالايدۇ.

مەنبە يەنە تۆۋەندىكى causes نى قاپلايدۇ:

## Prolonged Usage
Device نى ئۇزاق ئىشلىتىش:
- processor workload نى كۆپەيتىشى
- heat generation نى ئاشۇرۇشى

مۇمكىن.

## Background Applications

Too many/resource-heavy background apps:
- processor/system resources نى كۆپ ئىشلىتىدۇ
- heat نى ئاشۇرۇشى مۇمكىن

## Wi-Fi Hotspot

Source:
- device نى ئۇزاق ۋاقىت Wi-Fi hotspot قىلىپ ئىشلىتىش
- workload نى ئاشۇرۇپ overheating غا تۆھپە قوشۇشى مۇمكىن

## GPS / Other Connections

Source:
- GPS غا ئوخشاش connection/service لارنى unnecessary ئۇزاق ئىشلىتىش
- hardware strain نى ئاشۇرۇشى مۇمكىن

دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

**Exam memory: Mobile overheating → Battery health / prolonged use / background apps / hotspot / GPS**

---

# 6. Liquid Damage

## Source Symptoms

Liquid damage بولسا:
- screen ئاستىدا water/moisture كۆرۈنۈشى
- graphic/display distortion

بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

Visible sign بولمىسىمۇ:
- liquid exposure suspected بولسا caution كېرەك

## Source Action Flow

Transcript تۆۋەندىكى sequence نى بېرىدۇ:

1. **Device نى promptly power off قىلىڭ**
2. Visible liquid/moisture نى remove قىلىڭ
3. Internal component exposure suspected بولسا source thorough drying ئۈچۈن device disassembly نى تىلغا ئالىدۇ
4. Dry بولغاندىن كېيىن circuit boards ۋە contacts نى clean قىلىشنى source تەۋسىيە قىلىدۇ

**Source-safety/scope note:** بۇ reviewed lesson transcript نىڭ repair workflow ىنى study content سۈپىتىدە ساقلايدۇ. It does not add rice, heat-gun, chemical, charging, or model-specific disassembly instructions that are absent from the source.

**Exam memory: Suspected liquid damage → Power off first**

---

# 7. Broken / Cracked Screen

## Symptom

Screen:
- cracked
- shattered

بولسا:
- visibility تۆۋەنلەيدۇ
- usability تۆۋەنلەيدۇ
- physical safety risk بولۇشى مۇمكىن

## Source Action

- screen replacement نى consider قىلىش

## Important Source Check After Screen Work

Screen رايونىدا ياكى ئۇنىڭ ئەتراپىدا:
- webcam
- microphone
- wireless antennas

غا ئوخشاش components بار بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

Screen replacement/repair دىن كېيىن:
- بۇ functions operational مۇ؟

تەكشۈرۈش كېرەك.

**Exam memory: Broken screen → Replace screen + Verify nearby webcam/mic/antenna functions**

---

# 8. Digitizer / Touchscreen Problems

## Digitizer دېگەن نېمە؟

**Digitizer**
- touch input نى detect
- interpret

قىلىدىغان component.

## Symptom

Display image normal بولۇشى مۇمكىن، ئەمما:
- touch register بولمايدۇ
- touch inaccurate
- taps/icons response يوق
- virtual keyboard نى ئىشلىتىش قىيىن

## Source First Checks

- tablet/PC settings نى adjust/check قىلىڭ
- touchscreen functionality enabled مۇ؟
- properly configured مۇ؟

## If Still Unresponsive

Settings correct بولسىمۇ touch ئىشلىمىسە:
- more serious underlying hardware issue
- component repair/replacement

كېرەك بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Image works but touch fails → Digitizer/settings clue**

---

# 9. Stylus Not Working

Stylus problem digitizer problem غا ئوخشاپ كېتىشى مۇمكىن، چۈنكى device stylus input نى recognize قىلمايدۇ.

مەنبە closer troubleshooting دا stylus نىڭ ئۆزىنىمۇ تەكشۈرۈشنى تەكىتلەيدۇ.

## Possible Cause 1 — Stylus Has No Power

Some stylus:
- internal battery
- rechargeable power source

ئىشلىتىدۇ.

Battery depleted بولسا:
- stylus device بىلەن communication قىلالماسلىقى مۇمكىن

Source action:
- battery replace
- ياكى recharge

## Possible Cause 2 — Connectivity / Configuration

Some stylus:
- Bluetooth
- specific driver
- specific setting

تەلەپ قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

Check:
- paired مۇ؟
- configured مۇ؟
- required setting/driver enabled مۇ؟

## Possible Cause 3 — Physical Damage

Check:
- stylus tip damage
- internal damage

Source isolation method:
- stylus نى compatible device بىلەن test قىلىش

مەقسەت:
- stylus نىڭ ئۆزى repair/replacement كېرەكمۇ؟

ئايرىش.

**Exam memory: Stylus fails → Power → Pairing/config → Physical damage**

---

# 10. Cursor Drift / Touch Calibration

## Symptoms

### Cursor Drift
Cursor:
- ئۆزى drift قىلىدۇ
- erratic move قىلىدۇ
- precise selection قىيىنلىشىدۇ

### Touch Calibration Problem
Touch:
- intended target نى توغرا تاللىيالمايدۇ
- input inaccurate/inconsistent

## Source Cause

- digitizer misaligned
- digitizer inaccurate

## Source Action

> **Recalibrate the touchscreen digitizer**

Calibration:
- alignment نى correct قىلىش
- touch responsiveness/accuracy نى restore قىلىش

غا ياردەم بېرىدۇ.

**Exam memory: Cursor drift / inaccurate touch → Recalibrate digitizer**

---

# 11. Wi-Fi / Bluetooth — No Connectivity

## Symptom

- Wi-Fi connection يوق
- Bluetooth connection يوق

## Source First Checks

### 1. Adapter Enabled?
- wireless adapter enabled مۇ؟

### 2. Desired Network / Peripheral Correct?
- device correct network ياكى peripheral غا connect قىلىش ئۈچۈن configured مۇ؟

### 3. Antenna Connection
- antenna properly connected مۇ؟

### 4. Peripheral Battery
Wireless:
- mouse
- keyboard
- headset

غا ئوخشاش peripheral device لار sufficiently charged مۇ؟

Low battery:
- connectivity function نى توسۇشى مۇمكىن

**Exam memory: No wireless connection → Adapter → Configuration → Antenna → Peripheral battery**

---

# 12. Wi-Fi / Bluetooth — Poor or Intermittent Connectivity

## Source Checks

### Range
- device/peripheral reasonable range ئىچىدىمۇ؟

### Pairing / Configuration
- devices properly paired مۇ؟
- settings correct مۇ؟

### Interference
Potential interference:
- electronic devices
- physical obstacles

wireless signal نى disrupt قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Weak/intermittent wireless → Range + Pairing + Interference**

---

# 13. Malware / Rogue Applications

Malware:
- mobile-device functionality
- security
- privacy

غا خەۋپ پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Symptoms

### Unexpected Behavior
- sluggishness
- unresponsiveness

### Excessive Resource Use
Malicious software:
- system resource نى excessive consume قىلىشى مۇمكىن

### Unauthorized Data Usage
- unexpected mobile-data usage
- unexpected charges
- data allowance depletion

### Sensitive Function Access
Some malware:
- camera
- microphone
- location services

غا user consent بولمىغان ھالدا access قىلىشى مۇمكىن، دەپ source چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

## Source Troubleshooting Point

Unusual mobile-device behavior كۆرۈلسە:
- malware
- rogue application

نى possible cause سۈپىتىدە consider قىلىڭ.

**Source-boundary note:** transcript بۇ objective دا malware-removal utility ياكى reset/remediation procedure بەرمەيدۇ؛ reviewed lesson ئۇلارنى سىرتتىن قوشمايدۇ.

**Exam memory: Unexpected behavior + data/privacy activity → Consider malware/rogue app**

---

# 14. Degraded / Sluggish Mobile Performance

## Symptoms

- slow app launch
- delayed response
- overall unresponsiveness

## Possible Cause 1 — Too Many Background Apps

Background apps:
- memory
- processing resource

نى ئىشلىتىدۇ.

## Possible Cause 2 — Insufficient Available Storage

Mobile OS:
- temporary files
- application data

باشقۇرۇش ئۈچۈن free space غا ئېھتىياجلىق، دەپ source چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

Low free storage:
- performance issue پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Actions

- unnecessary applications نى close قىلىڭ
- storage space نى free up قىلىڭ

**Exam memory: Slow mobile device → Background apps + Low free storage**

---

# 15. Unable to Install New Applications

## Possible Cause 1 — Insufficient Storage

Device دا enough free space بولمىسا:
- OS new app download/install نى block قىلىشى مۇمكىن

Source storage consumers:
- existing applications
- photos
- videos
- cached data

## Possible Cause 2 — Installation Restrictions

Restrictions:
- device settings
- parental controls
- MDM — Mobile Device Management policies

دىن كېلىشى مۇمكىن.

Corporate environment دا:
- organizational data/device protection
- allowed application control

ئۈچۈن بۇ restriction لار ئىشلىتىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Actions

1. Device storage نى review قىلىڭ
2. Unnecessary files نى remove قىلىڭ
3. Unused applications نى uninstall قىلىڭ
4. Installation permission allowed مۇ؟
5. Administrative/MDM policy restriction بارمۇ؟

تەكشۈرۈڭ.

**Exam memory: App won't install → Free storage + Check permissions/restrictions**

---

# Objective 5.4 — Symptom → Cause / Action Table

| Symptom | Source-based clue/action |
|---|---|
| **Won’t/slowly charge** | Cable / port / debris / source-described power rating |
| **Battery drains / gets hot** | Aging / cycles / poor battery health |
| **Bulging device / swollen battery** | **Stop use + professional battery replacement** |
| **Port not working** | Test known-good/replacement cable |
| **Overheating** | Battery / long use / background apps / hotspot / GPS |
| **Liquid damage suspected** | **Power off first** |
| **Broken screen** | Replace; verify webcam/mic/antennas |
| **Image works, touch fails** | Digitizer/settings |
| **Stylus fails** | Battery/power / pairing/config / damage |
| **Cursor drift** | Recalibrate digitizer |
| **No Wi-Fi/Bluetooth** | Adapter / config / antenna / peripheral battery |
| **Intermittent wireless** | Range / pairing / interference |
| **Unexpected behavior/data use** | Malware / rogue app |
| **Sluggish mobile device** | Background apps / low storage |
| **Cannot install apps** | Low storage / settings / parental controls / MDM |

---

# Objective 5.4 — Final Exam Rules

1. **Check before replace.**
2. **Charging issue → cable, connection/port, debris, source-described power rating.**
3. **Swollen battery → stop using the device and seek professional replacement.**
4. **Suspected bad port → test another cable first.**
5. **Overheating → battery health + workload/background apps + hotspot/GPS.**
6. **Liquid suspected → power off first.**
7. **Broken screen repair → verify nearby webcam/mic/antenna functions.**
8. **Normal image + failed touch → digitizer/settings.**
9. **Stylus → power, pairing/config, physical condition.**
10. **Cursor drift → touchscreen/digitizer calibration.**
11. **No wireless → adapter/config/antenna/peripheral battery.**
12. **Poor wireless → range/pairing/interference.**
13. **Strange behavior/data/privacy activity → consider malware.**
14. **Sluggishness → background apps + storage.**
15. **Cannot install app → free space + permissions/restrictions/MDM.**

============================================================
Exam Objective 5.5
بىر ئەھۋال بېرىلگەندە، تور مەسىلىلىرىنى كاشىلا تۈگىتىڭ.

# Objective 5.5 — Troubleshooting Mindset

مەنبە يەنە بىر قېتىم مۇھىم CompTIA troubleshooting rule نى تەكىتلەيدۇ:

> **Repair ياكى component replacement قىلىشتىن بۇرۇن، verify / inspect / check قىلىڭ.**

Network troubleshooting دا:
- symptom نى توغرا تونۇش
- scope نى ئېنىقلاش
- software/configuration cause نى physical cause دىن ئايرىش
- connection، cable، interface ۋە network equipment نى systematic تەكشۈرۈش

مۇھىم.

Objective 5.5 دىكى reviewed flow:

1. Symptom
2. Possible source causes
3. Source checks/actions
4. Exam memory

---

# 1. Limited Connectivity / No Internet Access

## Symptom

User:
- Internet connection down
- ياكى **No Internet Access**

دېگەن message كۆرۈشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Check 1 — APIPA Address

**APIPA — Automatic Private IP Addressing**

مەنبە APIPA نى:
- DHCP server دىن IP address ئالالمىغاندا
- device ئۆزىگە self-assigned IP address بېرىدىغان mechanism

دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

ئەگەر device APIPA address ئالغان بولسا:
- DHCP server issue
- ياكى device network configuration issue

بارلىقىغا clue بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: APIPA → DHCP/configuration problem clue**

## Source Check 2 — Test Remote Connectivity

- remote device
- ياكى remote IP address

بىلەن communicate قىلىپ connection نى test قىلىڭ.

مەقسەت:
- problem local مۇ؟
- remote/network path تىمۇ؟

ئايرىشقا ياردەم بېرىش.

## Source Check 3 — Physical Causes

Software/configuration cause لارنى rule out قىلغاندىن كېيىن:
- faulty cable
- poor connection

غا ئوخشاش physical issue لارنى تەكشۈرۈڭ.

**Exam memory: Limited connectivity → APIPA → Test remote connectivity → Check cables/connections**

---

# 2. Slow Network Speeds

## Possible Cause 1 — Speed / Duplex Misconfiguration

Network interface:
- speed
- duplex

settings توغرا بولمىسا:
- packet loss
- transmission errors
- slow network speed

پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

Source action:
- NIC
- switch

settings network requirement غا ماس كېلەمدۇ تەكشۈرۈڭ.

**Exam memory: Slow wired network → Check speed/duplex**

## Possible Cause 2 — Damaged Cables / Poor Connections

Damaged cable:
- wear
- tear
- poor termination/connection

network performance نى تۆۋەنلىتىشى مۇمكىن.

Source action:
- cable damage نى inspect قىلىڭ
- connection secure مۇ؟

تەكشۈرۈڭ.

## Possible Cause 3 — Network Congestion

**Network congestion**
- data traffic network capacity دىن ئېشىپ كەتكەندە

پەيدا بولىدۇ.

Source effects:
- packet loss
- slower transmission

Possible congestion point:
- overloaded switch
- overloaded router

Source action:
- congestion point نى identify/address قىلىش

## Possible Cause 4 — Malware

Network device دا malware:
- bandwidth نى consume قىلىشى
- denial-of-service activity نى قوزغىتىشى

مۇمكىن، دەپ source چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

Source recommendations:
- antivirus software نى regularly update قىلىش
- network security audit ئېلىپ بېرىش

**Source-scope note:** reviewed lesson source نىڭ high-level malware/network-security wording ىنى ساقلايدۇ؛ specific antivirus product، scan command، IDS/IPS tuning ياكى incident-response procedure قوشمايدۇ.

## Determine the Scope

Slow-network troubleshooting نى تېزلىتىدىغان مۇھىم source step:

> **Problem scope نى ئېنىقلاڭ.**

Check:
- one device only?
- specific group of devices?
- entire network?

**Exam memory: Slow network → Scope first, then configuration/cable/congestion/malware**

---

# 3. Port Flapping

**Port flapping**
- switch ياكى router port/interface
- rapidly **up ↔ down**
- قايتا-قايتا on/off ھالەتكە ئۆتۈشى

بىلەن مۇناسىۋەتلىك network issue.

Effect:
- unstable connectivity
- repeated interruption

## Source-Stated Causes

### Physical Connection
- faulty cable
- faulty connector

### NIC Issue
- network interface card problem

**Exam memory: Port flapping = Interface repeatedly up/down → Cable/connector/NIC**

---

# 4. EMI — Electromagnetic Interference on Wired Networks

**EMI — Electromagnetic Interference**
- wired network signal stability غا دەخلى قىلىدىغان electromagnetic noise

Source examples:
- fluorescent lights
- power cables
- generators
- nearby electronic-device signals

## Effects

Cable EMI غا exposed بولسا:
- data signal disruption
- packet loss
- performance degradation

پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Mitigation

### Shielding
- shielded cable electromagnetic field effect نى ئازايتىشقا ياردەم بېرىدۇ

### Grounding
- proper grounding technique

### Cable Routing
- network cable نى power cable ۋە interference source دىن يىراق route قىلىش

**Exam memory: Wired EMI → Shielding + Grounding + Distance from power/interference sources**

---

# 5. Wireless Interference / EMI

Wireless network دا external interference source examples:

- microwave oven
- cordless telephone
- neighboring Wi-Fi signals

Source effects:
- intermittent connectivity
- slow data transfer
- poor network performance

## Source Mitigation

### Wi-Fi Channel Selection
- neighboring network/interference ئاز بولغان channel نى تاللاش

### Interference-Mitigation Features
Source advanced equipment example:
- signal filtering
- adaptive channel selection

**Source-framing caution:** transcript بۇ feature لارنى interference impact نى ئازايتالايدىغان technology examples سۈپىتىدە بېرىدۇ. Reviewed Uyghur ئۇلارنى guaranteed universal fix دەپ كۆرسەتمەيدۇ.

**Exam memory: Wireless interference → Channel + Environment + Equipment mitigation**

---

# 6. Intermittent Wireless Connectivity

## Symptom

Wireless connection:
- بىر ۋاقىت بار
- بىر ۋاقىت ئۈزۈلىدۇ
- signal strength inconsistent

## Possible Cause 1 — EMI / Interference

Previous section دىكى:
- electronics
- neighboring Wi-Fi
- other interference

## Possible Cause 2 — Physical Obstructions

Source examples:
- walls
- furniture
- other obstacles

Signal:
- weaken
- attenuate
- degrade

بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 3 — Distance

Client device access point دىن بەك يىراق بولسا:
- signal attenuation كۆپىيىدۇ

## Possible Cause 4 — Faulty / Outdated Hardware

Source examples:
- NIC
- access point

Malfunctioning/outdated hardware:
- stable connection قۇرالماسلىقى
- consistent signal strength ساقلىيالماسلىقى

مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Intermittent Wi-Fi → Interference + Obstacles + Distance + Hardware**

---

# 7. Latency

**Latency**
- data بىر network point دىن يەنە بىر point قا بېرىپ يېتىشكە كەتكەن ۋاقىت
- ئادەتتە **milliseconds (ms)** بىلەن ئۆلچىنىدۇ

Simple analogy:
- message ئەۋەتكەن بىلەن response ئالغان ئارىسىدىكى delay

## Source-Stated Causes

### Physical Distance
- device لار ئارىسىدىكى distance ئۇزۇن بولسا
- travel time كۆپىيىدۇ

### Network Congestion
- excessive traffic
- packet delay
- packet loss

latency نى ئاشۇرۇشى مۇمكىن.

### EMI / Other Slowdowns
Source:
- EMI
- network speed نى تۆۋەنلىتىدىغان باشقا factors

latency غا تەسىر قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Remedy Direction

- network configuration optimization
- hardware upgrade

overall network performance نى ياخشىلاشقا ياردەم بېرىشى مۇمكىن.

### Source-Model Caution

Transcript network speed ۋە latency نى «inverse» relationship قا ئوخشاش ئاددىي teaching wording بىلەن باغلايدۇ.

Reviewed Uyghur exam point:
- slow/congested/problematic network often experiences higher delay
- latency = delay metric

دەپ ساقلايدۇ.

بۇنى mathematically universal inverse law دەپ كېڭەيتمەيدۇ.

**Exam memory: Latency = How long packets take**

---

# 8. Jitter

**Jitter**
- network packet delay/latency نىڭ **variation**
- packet arrival timing نىڭ consistency سىنىڭ ئۆزگىرىشى

## Latency vs Jitter

### Latency
- overall / average delay

### Jitter
- that delay نىڭ ۋاقىت بويىچە قانچىلىك ئۆزگىرىدىغانلىقى

Source wording:
- latency = average delay between sending a packet and response
- jitter = delay variation over time

**Exam memory:**
> **Latency = Delay**
>
> **Jitter = Variation in delay**

---

# 9. Poor VoIP Quality

**VoIP — Voice over IP**
- voice data نى Internet/network ئارقىلىق real time دا يەتكۈزۈش

Real-time traffic بولغاچقا:
- latency
- jitter

غا تېخىمۇ sensitive.

## Source Symptoms

- audio breaking up
- voice distortion
- audio delay
- out-of-sync conversation
- audio dropouts

## Latency Effect

High latency:
- voice transmission delay
- conversation disjointed/out of sync

## Jitter Effect

High jitter:
- packet arrival timing inconsistent
- packets out of order
- distortion/dropouts

## Source Actions

- network configuration نى optimize قىلىش
- latency/jitter نى minimize قىلىش
- VoIP traffic نى باشقا data traffic دىن priority قىلىش
- routers/switches قا ئوخشاش network hardware/software نى upgrade قىلىش

**Exam memory: Poor VoIP = Latency + Jitter; source may prioritize voice traffic**

---

# 10. Authentication Failure

## Symptom

Device:
- wireless/secured network قا connect بولۇشقا تىرىشىدۇ
- required credential نى verify قىلالمايدۇ

## Possible Cause 1 — Wrong Password / Security Key

- incorrect network password
- incorrect security key

## Possible Cause 2 — Security-Method Mismatch

Device بىلەن network:
- different security setting

ئىشلىتىۋاتقان بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

Source examples:
- WPA2
- WPA3

## Source Actions

1. Correct password نى verify قىلىڭ
2. Proper wireless security protocol selected مۇ؟
3. Network قا reconnect قىلىڭ

**Exam memory: Authentication failure → Password/key + WPA2/WPA3 setting match**

---

# 11. Intermittent Internet Connectivity

## Symptom

Device:
- local network connection بار
- ئەمما Internet access قايتا-قايتا توختاپ/ئەسلىگە كېلىدۇ

User may see:
- website load بولماسلىق
- application connectivity loss
- online service temporary stop
- network icon/connection يەنىلا بار

## Source-Stated Causes

### Unstable ISP Connection
- Internet service provider side instability

### Network Congestion
- traffic overload

### Router Performance Issue
- router instability/performance

## Source Actions

- networking equipment نى restart قىلىش
- router configuration نى check قىلىش
- ISP بىلەن contact قىلىش

**Exam memory: Network connected but Internet drops → ISP / Congestion / Router**

---

# Objective 5.5 — Symptom → Cause / Action Table

| Symptom | Source-based clue/action |
|---|---|
| **Limited/no Internet** | APIPA/DHCP/config → remote test → cable/connection |
| **Slow network** | Scope → speed/duplex → cable → congestion → malware |
| **Port flapping** | Faulty cable/connector/NIC |
| **Wired EMI** | Shielding/grounding/routing |
| **Wireless interference** | Channels / interference sources / mitigation features |
| **Intermittent Wi-Fi** | EMI / obstacles / distance / NIC/AP hardware |
| **High latency** | Distance / congestion / EMI/network slowdown |
| **High jitter** | Variable packet delay |
| **Poor VoIP** | Latency + jitter |
| **Authentication failure** | Password/key / WPA2-WPA3 setting mismatch |
| **Internet repeatedly drops** | ISP / congestion / router performance |

---

# Objective 5.5 — Final Exam Rules

1. **Check before replace.**
2. **APIPA → suspect DHCP or network configuration.**
3. **Limited connectivity → test remote communication before moving to physical cabling.**
4. **Slow network → determine scope first.**
5. **Speed/duplex mismatch → packet loss/errors/slow speed.**
6. **Port flapping → interface repeatedly up/down; check cable/connector/NIC.**
7. **Wired EMI → shielding/grounding/routing away from interference.**
8. **Intermittent Wi-Fi → interference + obstacles + distance + hardware.**
9. **Latency = delay.**
10. **Jitter = variation in delay.**
11. **Poor VoIP → latency/jitter are key clues.**
12. **Authentication failure → password/security key + WPA2/WPA3 match.**
13. **Network connected but Internet drops → ISP/congestion/router.**

============================================================
Exam Objective 5.6
بىر ئەھۋال بېرىلگەندە، پرىنتېر مەسىلىلىرىنى كاشىلا تۈگىتىڭ.

# Objective 5.6 — Troubleshooting Mindset

مەنبە بۇ ئاخىرقى troubleshooting objective دا يەنە بىر قېتىم مۇھىم CompTIA rule نى تەكىتلەيدۇ:

> **Repair ياكى component replacement قىلىشتىن بۇرۇن، verify / inspect / check قىلىڭ.**

Printer troubleshooting دا ئەڭ مۇھىم بىرىنچى سوئال:

> **قايسى خىل printer بىلەن ئىشلەۋاتىسىز؟**

Source printer types:
- Inkjet
- Laser
- Thermal
- Impact

Printer technology ئوخشىمىغاچقا:
- symptom نىڭ cause ىمۇ
- maintenance/repair area سىمۇ

پەرقلىنىشى مۇمكىن.

Objective 5.6 دىكى reviewed flow:

1. Symptom
2. Printer type / affected area
3. Possible source causes
4. Source check/action
5. Exam memory

---

# 1. Vertical Lines / Streaks on Printed Pages

## Symptom

Printed page دا:
- unintended vertical lines
- streaks

كۆرۈنىدۇ.

مەنبە بىر printer symptom نىڭ كۆپ possible cause ى بولۇشى مۇمكىنلىكىنى تەكىتلەيدۇ.

## Laser Cause 1 — Debris on Photosensitive Drum

Laser printer دا:
- photosensitive/imaging drum ئۈستىدىكى debris

page دا repeating line/streak پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Laser Cause 2 — Scratched Imaging Drum

Drum rotates during printing.

Scratch:
- ھەر rotation دا similar line pattern نى قايتا ھاسىل قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Laser Cause 3 — Fuser Roller Wear / Damage

Fuser roller:
- worn
- damaged

بولسا fixing/fusing process جەريانىدا line پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Cause 4 — Dirty Feed Roller

Dirty feed roller:
- inconsistent paper feeding

پەيدا قىلىپ، source بويىچە printed page دا lines غا تۆھپە قوشۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Cause 5 — Scratched Scanner Glass

MFD/copier/scanner context دا:
- scanner glass scratched بولسا
- scanned/copied document دا streak/line پەيدا بولىدۇ
- كېيىن print قىلىنغان copy دا شۇ line كۆرۈنۈشى مۇمكىن

**Exam memory: Lines/streaks → Drum / Fuser / Feed roller / Scanner glass**

---

# 2. Garbled Print

## Symptom

Printed page دا:
- random characters
- incorrect characters
- unreadable/alien-symbol-like output

كۆرۈنىدۇ.

## Possible Cause 1 — Firmware Compatibility Problem

Recent firmware update:
- compatibility problem پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن

Source actions:
- firmware rollback نى consider قىلىش
- manufacturer support بىلەن contact قىلىش
- firmware patch بارمۇ تەكشۈرۈش
- firmware update نى reinstall قىلىش

## Possible Cause 2 — Corrupted / Incompatible Printer Driver

Computer دىكى printer driver:
- corrupted
- printer model بىلەن incompatible

بولسا garbled print پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

Source action:
- driver نى update
- ياكى reinstall

قىلىپ compatibility نى restore قىلىش.

## Possible Cause 3 — Communication Error

Computer ↔ printer communication:
- loose cable
- network issue
- interference

سەۋەبىدىن بۇزۇلۇشى مۇمكىن.

Source action:
- stable
- secure

connection بارلىقىنى verify قىلىڭ.

**Exam memory: Garbled print → Firmware / Driver / Communication**

---

# 3. Faded Prints

## Symptom

Output:
- faint
- washed out
- expected دەك crisp/vibrant ئەمەس

## Possible Cause 1 — Low Ink / Toner

Cartridge level:
- low

بولسا print intensity تۆۋەنلەيدۇ.

Source action:
- ink/toner level نى check قىلىڭ
- cartridge نى لازىم بولسا replace قىلىڭ

## Possible Cause 2 — Print Settings

Incorrect:
- print density
- resolution

settings faded output غا تۆھپە قوشۇشى مۇمكىن.

Source action:
- settings نى higher level غا adjust قىلىش

print quality نى ياخشىلاشقا ياردەم بېرىشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Faded print → Ink/Toner level + Density/Resolution settings**

---

# 4. Double Images / Echo Images

## Symptom

Printed document دا:
- duplicate image
- faint repeated image
- **echo image**

كۆرۈنىدۇ.

## Source First Check — Imaging Drum

Transcript بۇ symptom دا:
- imaging drum نى ئالدى بىلەن check قىلىشنى تەۋسىيە قىلىدۇ

Inspect for:
- visible damage
- wear
- contamination

**Source-accuracy note:** transcript drum نىڭ toner نى paper غا transfer قىلىش رولىنى ئاددىيلاشتۇرۇپ تەسۋىرلەيدۇ. Reviewed Uyghur source-supported troubleshooting action — **inspect the imaging drum** — نى ساقلايدۇ؛ laser transfer mechanism نى سىرتقى بىلىم بىلەن قايتا يېزىپ كەتمەيدۇ.

## Source Cleaning Guidance

Source:
- lint-free cloth
- isopropyl alcohol
- ياكى manufacturer direction

بويىچە drum cleaning نى تىلغا ئالىدۇ.

**Source-safety caution:** بۇ model-independent universal cleaning procedure دەپ قارالمايدۇ؛ manufacturer guidance نى ئالدىنقى ئورۇنغا قويۇڭ.

## Check Cleaning Mechanism

Printer cleaning mechanism:
- excess toner
- debris

نى internal component/drum دىن remove قىلىشقا ياردەم بېرىدۇ.

Source action:
- cleaning mechanism نى inspect قىلىڭ.

**Exam memory: Echo/double image → Imaging drum + Cleaning mechanism**

---

# 5. Speckling

## Symptom

Printed page دا:
- small toner/ink-like spots
- speckles

كۆپ كۆرۈنىدۇ.

## Possible Cause 1 — Toner Cartridge Leakage / Damage

Inspect toner cartridge:
- leak بارمۇ؟
- physical damage بارمۇ؟

Leakage:
- page speckling غا cause بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Dust / Debris / Excess Toner

Printer interior دا:
- dust
- debris
- excess toner

يىغىلىپ قالسا speckling پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Cleaning Areas

Source:
- drum
- fuser unit
- paper path

نى clean قىلىشنى تىلغا ئالىدۇ.

Source tools/material:
- toner-approved vacuum
- and/or lint-free cloth

**Exam memory: Speckling → Toner cartridge leak/damage + Internal toner/debris**

---

# 6. Toner Does Not Fuse Properly — Source Supplemental Laser Topic

مەنبە بۇ issue نى ئېنىق:

> **“may not be in the exam objectives, but it is important and specific to laser printers”**

دەپ ئايرىم ئەسكەرتىدۇ.

شۇڭا reviewed lesson بۇنى **source supplemental topic** سۈپىتىدەلا ساقلايدۇ، Objective 5.6 نىڭ guaranteed exam requirement ى دەپ ئوتتۇرىغا قويمايدۇ.

## Symptom

Toner paper غا توغرا fuse بولمىسا:
- smudging
- smearing
- poor print quality

پەيدا بولىدۇ.

## Possible Cause 1 — Faulty Fuser Unit

Fuser:
- heat
- pressure

ئارقىلىق toner نى paper غا bond قىلىدۇ.

Faulty fuser:
- toner fusion problem پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Incorrect Paper Type / Weight

Wrong:
- paper type
- paper weight

toner adhesion غا تەسىر قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

Source says:
- printer settings
- fuser temperature

adjustment بەزى ئەھۋالدا ياردەم بېرىشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 3 — Worn / Damaged Fuser Rollers

Roller wear/damage:
- proper toner fusion نى توسۇشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam/source memory: Smudging laser toner → Fuser + Paper type/weight + Fuser rollers**

---

# 7. Incorrect Page Orientation

## Symptom

Printed page:
- intended layout بىلەن ماس كەلمەيدۇ
- content wrong direction دا
- paper waste ياكى misalignment پەيدا بولىدۇ

## Common Source Cause

Wrong orientation selected:
- printer settings
- document software

## Source Action

Print قىلىشتىن بۇرۇن:
- orientation setting نى verify قىلىڭ

Correct option:
- **Portrait**
- ياكى **Landscape**

**Exam memory: Wrong page direction → Check Portrait/Landscape setting**

---

# 8. Tray Not Recognized

## Symptom

Printer:
- installed/loaded tray نى detect قىلالمايدۇ
- error message كۆرسىتىشى
- print نى refuse قىلىشى
- paper بار بولسىمۇ “insert paper” دەپ prompt قىلىشى مۇمكىن

## Possible Cause 1 — Tray Not Fully Seated

Tray:
- printer ئىچىگە تولۇق ئورۇنلاشمىغان بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Misconfigured Tray Settings

Tray configuration:
- wrong
- print job بىلەن mismatch

بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 3 — Tray Sensor Problem

Sensor:
- tray نى توغرا detect قىلالماسلىقى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 4 — Paper Type / Size Mismatch

Printer:
- specific tray دىن specific paper type/size نى كۈتۈۋاتقان بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

Tray configuration:
- print job setting بىلەن ماس كەلمىسە detection/printing issue پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Troubleshooting Order

1. Tray نى remove قىلىڭ
2. Tray نى **reseat** قىلىپ fully installed مۇ تەكشۈرۈڭ
3. Printer tray configuration نى verify قىلىڭ
4. Correct paper type/size assigned مۇ؟
5. Issue داۋاملاشسا tray sensor نى inspect قىلىڭ
6. Printer driver update ياردەم بېرىشى مۇمكىن

**Exam memory: Tray not recognized → Reseat → Config → Paper size/type → Sensor/driver**

---

# 9. Paper Not Feeding Properly

## Possible Cause 1 — Paper Alignment

Paper tray ئىچىدە paper:
- correctly aligned بولمىسا

feeding difficulty پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 2 — Wrong Paper Size / Weight

Loaded paper:
- printer supported size/weight specification غا ماس كەلمىسە

feeding problem پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 3 — Damaged / Moist Paper

Paper stack:
- creased
- damaged
- moist

بولسا feed problem پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Possible Cause 4 — Worn / Damaged Feed Roller

Feed roller:
- worn
- damaged

بولسا paper نى properly **grip** قىلالماسلىقى مۇمكىن.

**Transcript correction:** source `may not grit the paper properly` دەپ transcript قىلىنغان. Context بويىچە reviewed Uyghur canonical **grip the paper properly** مەنىسىنى ئىشلىتىدۇ.

**Exam memory: Paper won’t feed → Alignment + Size/Weight + Paper condition + Feed roller**

---

# 10. Multi-Page Misfeeds

## Symptom

Printer:
- بىر قېتىمدا بىر sheet ئەمەس
- multiple sheets نى بىللە تارتىدۇ

## Common Source Cause — Separation Pad

**Separation pad**
- بىر ۋاقىتتا پەقەت بىر sheet نى feed قىلىشقا ياردەم بېرىدۇ.

Worn/damaged separation pad:
- multi-page misfeed پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Multiple sheets at once → Separation pad**

---

# 11. Paper Jams

Paper jam:
- workflow نى ئۈزىدۇ
- printer غا damage risk پەيدا قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Safety First

Paper jam نى clear قىلىشتىن بۇرۇن:

1. **Printer نى service دىن چىقىرىڭ**
2. **Power source دىن disconnect قىلىڭ**

مەقسەت:
- electrical shock
- moving parts
- hot fuser burn

risk نى ئازايتىش.

## Source Jam-Clearing Flow

### 1. Inspect Paper Path
- obstruction نى locate قىلىڭ
- jammed paper/debris نى remove قىلىڭ

### 2. Remove Loose Pieces
- loose paper
- debris

paper movement نى توسۇۋاتامدۇ تەكشۈرۈڭ.

### 3. Frequent Jams → Pickup Roller

Pickup roller:
- wear
- contamination

بارمۇ inspect قىلىڭ.

Source says:
- clean
- ياكى replace

قىلىش future jams نى ئازايتىشقا ياردەم بېرىشى مۇمكىن.

### 4. Error Persists → Jam Sensor

Paper physically clear بولسىمۇ error message قالسا:
- paper path ئىچىدىكى jam sensor blocked بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Paper jam → Power disconnect → Clear path → Pickup roller → Jam sensor**

---

# 12. Finishing Unit Problems

**Finishing unit**
- بعض printer لاردا optional component
- basic printing دىن كېيىن additional finishing function تەمىنلەيدۇ.

Source examples:
- stapling
- hole punching
- binding
- folding

## Common Problem — Capacity Exceeded

Too many sheets:
- staple
- hole punch

قىلىشقا تىرىشىلسا:
- jam
- malfunction

پەيدا بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Action

1. Finishing unit maximum sheet capacity نى verify قىلىڭ
2. Print job page count نى لازىم بولسا reduce قىلىڭ
3. Jam بولغان بولسا carefully clear قىلىڭ
4. Printer نى restart قىلىش normal operation نى restore قىلىشى مۇمكىن

**Exam memory: Finisher jam → Check maximum sheet capacity**

---

# 13. Grinding Noise

## Symptom

Printer دىن:
- grinding noise

ئاڭلىنىدۇ.

## Source Meaning

Grinding noise:
- mechanical issue clue

بولۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Source Inspection Areas

Printer cover نى ئېچىپ visual inspect:
- gears
- rollers
- belts

Check for:
- damage
- wear
- misalignment

## Preventive Maintenance — Source

- printer interior cleaning
- moving parts lubrication

**manufacturer guidelines** بويىچە قىلىنىدۇ.

**Exam memory: Grinding printer → Inspect gears / rollers / belts**

---

# 14. Backed-Up / Frozen Print Queue

## Symptom

Multiple print jobs:
- queue دا waiting
- ئەمما print بولمايدۇ

## Source-Stated Causes

### Printer Out of Consumables
- paper يوق
- ink يوق
- toner يوق

Current job تاماملانمىسا:
- later jobs queue دا يىغىلىدۇ.

### Printer Offline / Connectivity Problem
- printer offline
- communication issue

بولسا jobs accumulate قىلىدۇ.

### Error in One Print Job
Specific job:
- processing نى interrupt قىلىپ
- other jobs نى pending قالدۇرۇشى مۇمكىن.

## Windows Source Workflow

Transcript Windows دا تۆۋەندىكى sequence نى بېرىدۇ:

1. **Settings** نى ئېچىڭ
2. **Printers** قا بېرىڭ
3. Printer نى تاللاپ **print queue** نى كۆرۈڭ
4. Current job نى restart قىلىپ بېقىڭ
5. Job داۋاملىق fail بولسا queue دىن delete قىلىڭ
6. قايتا print قىلىپ بېقىڭ

## Completely Frozen Queue — Print Spooler

ئەگەر:
- job remove بولمايدۇ
- queue completely frozen

بولسا source:
- **print spooler service** نى stop
- ئاندىن restart

قىلىش كېرەك بولۇشى مۇمكىن، دەپ چۈشەندۈرىدۇ.

**Source-scope note:** reviewed lesson source دىكى Windows-level workflow نى ساقلايدۇ، ئەمما command-line spooler commands ياكى registry/service-recovery procedures قوشمايدۇ.

**Exam memory: Queue backed up → Consumables/offline/job error → Queue → Spooler if frozen**

---

# 15. Printer Connectivity Issues

## Symptom

Print job:
- printer غا يېتىپ بارمايدۇ
- network printer/print server بىلەن communication بولمايدۇ

## Source-Stated Causes

- incorrect network settings
- disconnected cable
- wireless signal problem
- printer powered off
- printer offline

## Source Troubleshooting Flow

### 1. Power
- printer powered on مۇ؟

### 2. Network Connection
- network قا properly connected مۇ؟

### 3. Correct Printer Selected
- user/computer correct printer نى تاللىغانمۇ؟

### 4. Network Configuration
- printer IP address
- network configuration

ئۆزگىرىپ كەتمىگەنمۇ؟

### 5. Restart
Source says:
- printer
- computer
- network equipment

restart قىلىش communication نى restore قىلىشى مۇمكىن.

**Exam memory: Print job cannot reach printer → Power → Connection → Correct printer → IP/config → Restart**

---

# Objective 5.6 — Symptom → Cause / Action Table

| Symptom | Source-based clue/action |
|---|---|
| **Vertical lines/streaks** | Drum / fuser / feed roller / scanner glass |
| **Garbled characters** | Firmware / driver / communication |
| **Faded print** | Low ink/toner / density-resolution settings |
| **Double/echo image** | Imaging drum / cleaning mechanism |
| **Speckling** | Toner cartridge leak/damage / internal debris |
| **Toner smudges** | Supplemental source topic: fuser / paper type-weight / fuser roller |
| **Wrong orientation** | Portrait/Landscape setting |
| **Tray not recognized** | Reseat / tray config / paper type-size / sensor / driver |
| **Paper won’t feed** | Alignment / paper spec-condition / feed roller |
| **Multiple sheets feed** | Separation pad |
| **Paper jam** | Disconnect power → paper path → pickup roller → jam sensor |
| **Finisher jam** | Maximum sheet capacity |
| **Grinding noise** | Gears / rollers / belts |
| **Print queue backed up** | Consumables / offline / job error / spooler |
| **Printer unreachable** | Power / cable-wireless / selection / IP-config / restart |

---

# Objective 5.6 — Final Exam Rules

1. **Check before replace.**
2. **Printer type matters first.**
3. **Lines/streaks → drum/fuser/feed roller/scanner glass.**
4. **Garbled print → firmware/driver/communication.**
5. **Faded print → ink/toner + print settings.**
6. **Echo image → imaging drum/cleaning mechanism.**
7. **Speckling → toner cartridge + internal debris.**
8. **Toner not fusing is source-labeled supplemental; check fuser/paper/rollers.**
9. **Wrong page direction → Portrait/Landscape.**
10. **Tray not recognized → reseat + configuration + sensor.**
11. **Paper feed → alignment/spec/condition/feed roller.**
12. **Multi-feed → separation pad.**
13. **Paper jam → disconnect power before clearing the paper path.**
14. **Finisher jam → check sheet-capacity limit.**
15. **Grinding → gears/rollers/belts.**
16. **Frozen print queue → job/queue; spooler may need restart.**
17. **Printer connectivity → power/network/selected printer/IP configuration.**
